ARCHIVO: .\admin.html
================================================================================
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="es">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <title>Admin</title>

  <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/sweetalert2@11"></script>

</head>

<body class="bg-gray-100">

  <!-- NAVBAR -->
  <div class="bg-white shadow p-4 flex justify-between items-center">

    <h1 class="font-bold text-lg">Panel Admin</h1>

    <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
      <span id="userName" class="text-gray-600"></span>

      <button onclick="logout()"
        class="bg-red-500 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded hover:bg-red-600">
        Cerrar sesión
      </button>
    </div>

  </div>

  <!-- CONTENIDO -->
  <div class="p-6">

    <!-- BUSCADOR -->
    <input 
      type="text" 
      id="search"
      placeholder="Buscar usuario..."
      class="w-full mb-4 p-2 border rounded"
    >

    <!-- TABLA -->
    <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-4 overflow-x-auto">

      <table class="w-full text-left">
        <thead>
          <tr class="border-b text-gray-600">
            <th class="p-2">Usuario</th>
            <th class="p-2">Estado</th>
            <th class="p-2">Acciones</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody id="usersTable"></tbody>
      </table>

      <p id="loading" class="text-center text-gray-400 mt-4">
        Cargando usuarios...
      </p>

    </div>

  </div>

  <!-- AUTH GUARD -->
  <script type="module">
    import { requireAdmin } from "./js/auth.guard.js";
    requireAdmin();
  </script>

  <!-- SCRIPT -->
  <script type="module">

    import { db, auth } from "./js/firebase/firebase.init.js";

    import {
      collection,
      getDocs,
      doc,
      updateDoc,
      deleteDoc
    } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-firestore.js";

    import {
      onAuthStateChanged,
      signOut
    } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-auth.js";

    const table = document.getElementById("usersTable");
    const searchInput = document.getElementById("search");
    const loading = document.getElementById("loading");

    let users = [];

    // 🔥 Mostrar usuario
    onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user)=>{
      if(user){
        document.getElementById("userName").innerText =
          user.email.replace("@app.com","");
      }
    });

    // 🚀 Cargar usuarios
    async function loadUsers(){

      loading.style.display = "block";
      table.innerHTML = "";

      const snapshot = await getDocs(collection(db,"users"));

      users = [];

      snapshot.forEach(docSnap => {
        users.push({
          id: docSnap.id,
          ...docSnap.data()
        });
      });

      renderUsers(users);

      loading.style.display = "none";
    }

    // 🎯 Render
    function renderUsers(list){

      table.innerHTML = "";

      list.forEach(user => {

        table.innerHTML += `
          <tr class="border-b hover:bg-gray-50">
            <td class="p-2">${user.username}</td>

            <td class="p-2">
              <span class="px-2 py-1 rounded text-white text-sm
                ${user.enabled ? "bg-green-500" : "bg-red-500"}">
                ${user.enabled ? "Activo" : "Inactivo"}
              </span>
            </td>

            <td class="p-2 flex gap-2">

              <button onclick="toggleUser('${user.id}', ${user.enabled})"
                class="bg-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-600 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded">
                ${user.enabled ? "Desactivar" : "Activar"}
              </button>

              <button onclick="deleteUser('${user.id}')"
                class="bg-red-500 hover:bg-red-600 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded">
                Eliminar
              </button>

            </td>
          </tr>
        `;
      });

    }

    // 🔍 BUSCADOR
    searchInput.addEventListener("input", ()=>{

      const value = searchInput.value.toLowerCase();

      const filtered = users.filter(u =>
        u.username.toLowerCase().includes(value)
      );

      renderUsers(filtered);
    });

    // 🔄 Toggle
    async function toggleUser(id, current){

      await updateDoc(doc(db,"users",id),{
        enabled: !current
      });

      Swal.fire({
        toast:true,
        position:"top-end",
        icon:"success",
        title:"Usuario actualizado",
        showConfirmButton:false,
        timer:2000
      });

      loadUsers();
    }

    // ❌ Delete
    async function deleteUser(id){

      const confirm = await Swal.fire({
        title:"¿Eliminar usuario?",
        icon:"warning",
        showCancelButton:true
      });

      if(confirm.isConfirmed){

        await deleteDoc(doc(db,"users",id));

        Swal.fire("Eliminado","","success");

        loadUsers();
      }

    }

    // 🚪 Logout
    async function logout(){
      await signOut(auth);
      window.location.href = "login.html";
    }

    // GLOBAL
    window.toggleUser = toggleUser;
    window.deleteUser = deleteUser;
    window.logout = logout;

    loadUsers();

  </script>

</body>
</html>

<script>
    (function() {
      const statusEl = document.getElementById('networkStatus');
      if (!statusEl) return;
      
      function updateNetworkStatus() {
        if (navigator.onLine) {
          statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-emerald-500 rounded-full animate-pulse"></span> Online';
        } else {
          statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-red-500 rounded-full"></span> Offline';
        }
      }
      
      window.addEventListener('online', updateNetworkStatus);
      window.addEventListener('offline', updateNetworkStatus);
      updateNetworkStatus();
    })();
  </script>
    <script type="module" src="js/agencies/agencies.page.js"></script>
</body>
</html>

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="es">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <meta name="theme-color" content="#2563eb" />
  <title>Gestión de Archivos</title>

  <link rel="icon" href="assets/icons/Logo-vertical-azul.png" />

  <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>

  <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.5.0/css/all.min.css" />

  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/sweetalert2@11"></script>
</head>
<body class="bg-gray-50 flex items-center justify-center min-h-screen p-4">

  <div class="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-200 overflow-hidden">
    
    <div class="p-5 border-b border-gray-100 bg-gray-50/50">
        <h3 class="text-sm font-bold text-gray-800 flex items-center gap-2">
            <i class="fas fa-database text-blue-600"></i>
            Gestión de Agencias
        </h3>
        <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">Exporta o importa la base de datos en formato JSON.</p>
        <a href="index.html">Volver</a>
    </div>

    <div class="p-5 space-y-3">
        <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <button 
                id="btnExport" 
                class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 p-4 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl hover:bg-blue-50 hover:border-blue-200 transition-all group active:scale-95"
            >
                <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-blue-100 flex items-center justify-center text-blue-600 group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform">
                    <i class="fas fa-file-export"></i>
                </div>
                <span class="text-xs font-bold text-gray-700">Exportar</span>
            </button>

            <button 
                id="btnImport" 
                class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 p-4 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl hover:bg-emerald-50 hover:border-emerald-200 transition-all group active:scale-95"
            >
                <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-emerald-100 flex items-center justify-center text-emerald-600 group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform">
                    <i class="fas fa-file-import"></i>
                </div>
                <span class="text-xs font-bold text-gray-700">Importar</span>
            </button>
        </div>

        <input type="file" id="fileImport" accept=".json" class="hidden" />
    </div>

    <div class="px-5 py-3 bg-gray-50 border-t border-gray-100 flex justify-center">
        <span class="text-[10px] text-gray-400 font-medium uppercase tracking-widest">Sistema de Control v1.0</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <script type="module">
    import { requireAuth } from "./js/auth.guard.js";
  
    requireAuth();
  </script>

  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/idb@8/build/umd.js"></script>
  
  <script type="module">
    import { exportAgenciesAdvanced, importAgenciesAdvanced } from './js/agencies/agencies.store.js';
    
    const btnExport = document.getElementById('btnExport');
    const btnImport = document.getElementById('btnImport');
    const fileImport = document.getElementById('fileImport');

    // Función de ayuda para notificaciones tipo Toast
    const Toast = Swal.mixin({
      toast: true,
      position: 'top-end',
      showConfirmButton: false,
      timer: 3000,
      timerProgressBar: true
    });

    // Acción Exportar
    btnExport.onclick = async () => {
      try {
        await exportAgenciesAdvanced();
        Toast.fire({ icon: 'success', title: 'Archivo exportado correctamente' });
      } catch (err) {
        Toast.fire({ icon: 'error', title: 'Error al exportar' });
      }
    };
    
    // Acción Abrir Selector de Archivos
    btnImport.onclick = () => fileImport.click();
    
    // Acción Importar al cambiar archivo
    fileImport.addEventListener('change', async (e) => {
      const file = e.target.files[0];
      if (file) {
        try {
          await importAgenciesAdvanced(file);
          Swal.fire({
            icon: 'success',
            title: '¡Importación exitosa!',
            text: 'Los datos han sido cargados.',
            confirmButtonColor: '#2563eb'
          });
        } catch (err) {
          Swal.fire({
            icon: 'error',
            title: 'Fallo la carga',
            text: 'El archivo JSON no es válido.'
          });
        }
        // Limpiar para poder subir el mismo archivo después si se desea
        fileImport.value = '';
      }
    });
  </script>

</body>
</html>

ARCHIVO: .\data.json

                            <div class="check-icon opacity-0 absolute top-2 right-2 text-blue-600 font-bold text-xs">✓</div>
                        </div>
                    </label>

                    <label class="relative cursor-pointer">
                        <input type="checkbox" id="toallita" class="hidden product-check">
                        <div class="product-card border-2 border-slate-100 rounded-2xl p-4 text-center transition-all hover:border-slate-300">
                            <span class="text-3xl block mb-2">🧻</span>
                            <span class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700">Toallita</span>
                            <div class="check-icon opacity-0 absolute top-2 right-2 text-blue-600 font-bold text-xs">✓</div>
                        </div>
                    </label>
                </div>

                <button onclick="guardar()" 
                    class="w-full bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 active:scale-95 text-white py-4 rounded-2xl font-bold text-lg shadow-lg shadow-indigo-200 transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                    <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="h-5 w-5" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
                        <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9 5l7 7-7 7" />
                    </svg>
                    Registrar Entrega
                </button>
            </div>

            <div class="bg-slate-50 border-t border-slate-100 p-6">
                <h3 class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase mb-4 tracking-wider">📊 Resumen General</h3>
                <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    <div class="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-100">
                        <div class="text-xs text-slate-500">Kits Completos</div>
                        <div class="text-2xl font-bold text-indigo-600" id="r_kits">0</div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-100">
                        <div class="text-xs text-slate-500">Mistolin</div>
                        <div class="text-xl font-semibold" id="r_mistolin">0</div>

                    <div class="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-100">
                        <div class="text-xs text-slate-500">Cloro</div>
                        <div class="text-xl font-semibold" id="r_cloro">0</div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-100">
                        <div class="text-xs text-slate-500">Ace / Toallitas</div>

    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/sweetalert2@11"></script>

    <script>
let registros = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("entregas")) || [];
let editIndex = null; // Para saber si estamos editando o creando

async function guardar() {
    const agenciaInput = document.getElementById("agencia");
    const agencia = agenciaInput.value;

    const data = {
        agencia,
        mistolin: document.getElementById("mistolin").checked,
        cloro: document.getElementById("cloro").checked,
        ace: document.getElementById("ace").checked,
        toallita: document.getElementById("toallita").checked,
        fecha: new Date().toLocaleString('es-DO', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', day: '2-digit', month: 'short' })
    };

    // Validación con SweetAlert2
    if (!agencia) {
        Swal.fire({
            icon: 'error',
            title: 'Faltan datos',
            text: 'Por favor, ingresa el nombre de la agencia.',
            confirmButtonColor: '#4f46e5'
        });
        return;
    }

    if (!data.mistolin && !data.cloro && !data.ace && !data.toallita) {
        Swal.fire({
            icon: 'warning',
            title: 'Carrito vacío',
            text: 'Selecciona al menos un producto para registrar.',
            confirmButtonColor: '#4f46e5'
        });
        return;
    }

    // Lógica para Guardar o Actualizar
    if (editIndex !== null) {
        registros[editIndex] = data;
        editIndex = null;
        Swal.fire({
            icon: 'success',
            title: '¡Actualizado!',
            text: 'El registro se modificó correctamente.',
            timer: 1500,
            showConfirmButton: false
        });
    } else {
        registros.push(data);
        Swal.fire({
            icon: 'success',
            title: '¡Registrado!',
            text: 'La entrega ha sido guardada.',
            timer: 1500,
            showConfirmButton: false
        });
    }

    localStorage.setItem("entregas", JSON.stringify(registros));
    render();
    limpiar();
}

function prepararEdicion(index) {
    // Buscamos el registro (considerando que el render está invertido)
    const actualIndex = registros.length - 1 - index;
    const r = registros[actualIndex];

    document.getElementById("agencia").value = r.agencia;
    document.getElementById("mistolin").checked = r.mistolin;
    document.getElementById("cloro").checked = r.cloro;
    document.getElementById("ace").checked = r.ace;
    document.getElementById("toallita").checked = r.toallita;

    editIndex = actualIndex;
    
    // Scroll hacia arriba para que el usuario vea que está editando
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
}

function eliminarRegistro(index) {
    const actualIndex = registros.length - 1 - index;
    
    Swal.fire({
        title: '¿Estás seguro?',
        text: "No podrás revertir esta acción",
        icon: 'warning',
        showCancelButton: true,
        confirmButtonColor: '#ef4444',
        cancelButtonColor: '#64748b',
        confirmButtonText: 'Sí, eliminar',
        cancelButtonText: 'Cancelar'
    }).then((result) => {
        if (result.isConfirmed) {
            registros.splice(actualIndex, 1);
            localStorage.setItem("entregas", JSON.stringify(registros));
            render();
            Swal.fire('Eliminado', 'El registro ha sido borrado.', 'success');
        }
    });
}

function render() {
    const lista = document.getElementById("lista");
    lista.innerHTML = "";
    let total = { mistolin: 0, cloro: 0, ace: 0, toallita: 0, kits: 0 };

    registros.slice().reverse().forEach((r, index) => {
        if (r.mistolin) total.mistolin++;
        if (r.cloro) total.cloro++;
        if (r.ace) total.ace++;
        if (r.toallita) total.toallita++;
        if (r.mistolin && r.cloro && r.ace && r.toallita) total.kits++;

        lista.innerHTML += `
            <div class="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-slate-100 flex justify-between items-center">
                <div onclick="prepararEdicion(${index})" class="cursor-pointer flex-1">
                    <div class="font-bold text-slate-800">${r.agencia} <span class="text-indigo-500 text-[10px] ml-2">✎ Editar</span></div>
                    <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-medium uppercase mt-1">${r.fecha}</div>
                    <div class="flex gap-1 mt-2">
                        ${r.mistolin ? "<span>🧴</span>" : ""} 
                        ${r.cloro ? "<span>🧪</span>" : ""} 
                        ${r.ace ? "<span>🧺</span>" : ""} 
                        ${r.toallita ? "<span>🧻</span>" : ""}
                    </div>
                </div>
                <button onclick="eliminarRegistro(${index})" class="ml-4 text-slate-300 hover:text-red-500 transition-colors">
                    <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="h-5 w-5" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
                        <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16" />
                    </svg>
                </button>
            </div>
        `;
    });

    // Actualizar números del dashboard
    document.getElementById("r_mistolin").textContent = total.mistolin;
    document.getElementById("r_cloro").textContent = total.cloro;
    document.getElementById("r_ace").textContent = total.ace;
    document.getElementById("r_toallita").textContent = total.toallita;
    document.getElementById("r_kits").textContent = total.kits;
}

function limpiar() {
    document.getElementById("agencia").value = "";
    document.querySelectorAll(".product-check").forEach(c => c.checked = false);
    editIndex = null;
}

render();
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    const statusEl = document.getElementById('networkStatus');
    function updateNetworkStatus() {
      if (!statusEl) return;
      if (navigator.onLine) {
        statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-emerald-500 rounded-full animate-pulse"></span> Online';
      } else {
        statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-red-500 rounded-full"></span> Offline';
      }
    }
    window.addEventListener('online', updateNetworkStatus);
    window.addEventListener('offline', updateNetworkStatus);
    updateNetworkStatus();

    import { signOut } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-auth.js";
    
    window.logout = async () => {
      await signOut(auth);
      window.location.href = "login.html";
    };

  <!-- Menú móvil -->
  <script>
    (function() {
      const mobileMenu = document.getElementById('mobileMenu');
      const openBtn = document.getElementById('mobileMenuBtn');
      const closeBtn = document.getElementById('closeMenuBtn');
      
      if (openBtn && mobileMenu) {
        openBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          mobileMenu.classList.remove('hidden');
          document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        });
        
        const closeMenu = () => {
          mobileMenu.classList.add('hidden');
          document.body.style.overflow = '';
        };
        
        if (closeBtn) closeBtn.addEventListener('click', closeMenu);
        mobileMenu.addEventListener('click', (e) => { if (e.target === mobileMenu) closeMenu(); });
      }
    })();
  </script>

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="es">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, user-scalable=no">
  <title>Login | Control de Agencias</title>

  <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/sweetalert2@11"></script>

  <style>
    @import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@300;400;500;600;700&display=swap');
    
    * { margin: 0; padding: 0; box-sizing: border-box; }
    
    body {
      font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #667eea 0%, #764ba2 100%);
      min-height: 100vh;
    }
    
    @keyframes fadeInUp {
      from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(30px); }
      to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); }
    }
    
    .login-card { animation: fadeInUp 0.6s ease-out; }
    
    .input-focus:focus {
      box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.2);
      border-color: #4f46e5;
    }
    
    .loader {
      display: inline-block;
      width: 20px;
      height: 20px;
      border: 2px solid #fff;
      border-radius: 50%;
      border-top-color: transparent;
      animation: spin 0.6s linear infinite;
    }
    
    @keyframes spin { to { transform: rotate(360deg); } }
  </style>

<body class="flex items-center justify-center p-4">
  <div class="login-card w-full max-w-md">
    
    <div class="text-center mb-8">
      <div class="w-20 h-20 bg-white/10 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4 backdrop-blur">
        <svg class="w-12 h-12 text-white" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5" d="M19 21V5a2 2 0 00-2-2H7a2 2 0 00-2 2v16m14 0h2m-2 0h-5m-9 0H3m2 0h5M9 7h1m-1 4h1m4-4h1m-1 4h1m-5 10v-5a1 1 0 011-1h2a1 1 0 011 1v5m-4 0h4"></path>
        </svg>
      </div>
      <h1 class="text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-1">Control de Agencias</h1>
      <p class="text-indigo-200 text-sm">Inicia sesión para continuar</p>
    </div>

    <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl p-8">
      <h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800 text-center mb-6">Bienvenido</h2>

      <form id="loginForm" class="space-y-5">
        <div>
          <label class="block text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 mb-2">
            <i class="fas fa-user mr-2 text-indigo-500"></i>
            Usuario
          </label>
          <input 
            type="text" 
            id="username" 
            name="username"
            autocomplete="username"
            placeholder="Ingresa tu usuario"
            class="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-gray-300 rounded-xl focus:outline-none input-focus transition-all text-gray-700"
            autofocus
          >
        </div>

        <div>
          <label class="block text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 mb-2">
            <i class="fas fa-lock mr-2 text-indigo-500"></i>
            Contraseña
          </label>
          <div class="relative">
            <input 
              type="password" 
              id="password" 
              name="password"
              autocomplete="current-password"
              placeholder="••••••"
              class="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-gray-300 rounded-xl focus:outline-none input-focus transition-all text-gray-700 pr-12"
            >
            <button type="button" id="togglePassword" class="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 hover:text-indigo-600 transition-colors">
              <i class="fas fa-eye-slash" id="toggleIcon"></i>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <button type="submit" id="loginBtn" class="w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-indigo-700 hover:from-indigo-700 hover:to-indigo-800 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl transition-all transform active:scale-95 shadow-lg shadow-indigo-200 flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
          <i class="fas fa-sign-in-alt"></i>
          <span>Iniciar Sesión</span>
        </button>

        <div class="relative my-6">
          <div class="absolute inset-0 flex items-center">
            <div class="w-full border-t border-gray-200"></div>
          </div>
          <div class="relative flex justify-center text-sm">
            <span class="px-4 bg-white text-gray-400">¿Nuevo usuario?</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <a href="register.html" class="block text-center text-indigo-600 hover:text-indigo-700 font-semibold transition-colors">
          Crear una cuenta
        </a>
      </form>
    </div>

    <p class="text-center text-indigo-200 text-xs mt-6">Sistema de Control de Agencias v2.5</p>
  </div>

  <script type="module">
    import { auth, db } from "./js/firebase/firebase.init.js";

    import {
      signInWithEmailAndPassword,
      onAuthStateChanged
    } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-auth.js";

    import {
      doc,
      getDoc
    } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-firestore.js";

    // ============================================
    // 🧹 LIMPIEZA INTELIGENTE DE DATOS LOCALES
    // ============================================
    async function limpiarDatosLocales() {
      console.log('🧹 Limpiando cachés locales...');
      
      // 1. Limpiar localStorage (excepto configuraciones importantes)
      const keepKeys = ['user_preferences', 'theme']; // Cosas que queremos conservar
      const keysToDelete = [];
      
      for (let i = 0; i < localStorage.length; i++) {
        const key = localStorage.key(i);
        if (!keepKeys.includes(key)) {
          keysToDelete.push(key);
        }
      }
      
      keysToDelete.forEach(key => {
        localStorage.removeItem(key);
        console.log(`   - Eliminado localStorage: ${key}`);
      });
      
      // 2. Limpiar sessionStorage completamente
      if (sessionStorage.length > 0) {
        sessionStorage.clear();
        console.log('   - SessionStorage limpiado');
      }
      
      // 3. Limpiar IndexedDB (solo cachés, NO datos de agencias)
      try {
        // Abrir la base de datos
        const request = indexedDB.open('control_agencias_db');
        
        request.onsuccess = (event) => {
          const db = event.target.result;
          
          // Lista de stores a limpiar (NO 'agencies' ni 'observaciones')
          const storesToClear = ['sync_queue', 'findings', 'reports', 'visits', 'observations', 'routes'];
          
          storesToClear.forEach(storeName => {
            if (db.objectStoreNames.contains(storeName)) {
              const transaction = db.transaction(storeName, 'readwrite');
              const store = transaction.objectStore(storeName);
              const clearRequest = store.clear();
              
              clearRequest.onsuccess = () => {
                console.log(`   - IndexedDB: ${storeName} limpiado`);
              };
              clearRequest.onerror = (err) => {
                console.warn(`   - Error limpiando ${storeName}:`, err);
              };
            }
          });
          
          db.close();
        };
        
        request.onerror = (err) => {
          console.warn('   - Error abriendo IndexedDB:', err);
        };
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('   - IndexedDB no disponible:', err);
      }
      
      // 4. Limpiar caché de mapas (si existe)
      if ('caches' in window) {
        try {
          const cacheNames = await caches.keys();
          for (const cacheName of cacheNames) {
            if (cacheName.includes('leaflet') || cacheName.includes('tile')) {
              await caches.delete(cacheName);
              console.log(`   - Caché de mapas eliminado: ${cacheName}`);
            }
          }
        } catch (err) {
          console.warn('   - Error limpiando cachés:', err);
        }
      }
      
      console.log('✅ Limpieza de cachés completada');
    }
    
    // Ejecutar limpieza al cargar la página
    await limpiarDatosLocales();
    
    // Mostrar mensaje de confirmación (opcional, solo si hay algo que limpiar)
    const toast = Swal.mixin({
      toast: true,
      position: 'top-end',
      showConfirmButton: false,
      timer: 2000,
      timerProgressBar: true
    });
    
    toast.fire({
      icon: 'info',
      title: 'Caché limpiado',
      text: 'Datos temporales eliminados'
    });
    
    // ============================================
    // LOGIN NORMAL
    // ============================================
    
    const usernameInput = document.getElementById('username');
    const passwordInput = document.getElementById('password');
    const loginBtn = document.getElementById('loginBtn');
    const loginForm = document.getElementById('loginForm');
    const togglePasswordBtn = document.getElementById('togglePassword');
    const toggleIcon = document.getElementById('toggleIcon');

    let isLoading = false;

    onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user) => {
      if (user && !isLoading) {
        window.location.href = "index.html";
      }
    });

    togglePasswordBtn?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const type = passwordInput.getAttribute('type') === 'password' ? 'text' : 'password';
      passwordInput.setAttribute('type', type);
      toggleIcon.className = type === 'password' ? 'fas fa-eye-slash' : 'fas fa-eye';
    });

    function sanitizeInput(str) {
      if (!str) return '';
      return str.trim().replace(/[<>]/g, '');
    }

    function setLoading(loading) {
      isLoading = loading;
      if (loading) {
        loginBtn.disabled = true;
        loginBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="loader"></span><span class="ml-2">Verificando...</span>';
      } else {
        loginBtn.disabled = false;
        loginBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-sign-in-alt"></i><span>Iniciar Sesión</span>';
      }
    }

    function showToast(icon, title) {
      Swal.fire({
        toast: true,
        position: "top-end",
        icon: icon,
        title: title,
        showConfirmButton: false,
        timer: 2500,
        timerProgressBar: true
      });
    }

    async function handleLogin(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      
      if (isLoading) return;
      
      let username = sanitizeInput(usernameInput.value);
      let password = sanitizeInput(passwordInput.value);
      
      if (!username) {
        showToast('warning', 'Ingresa tu usuario');
        usernameInput.focus();
        return;
      }
      
      if (!password) {
        showToast('warning', 'Ingresa tu contraseña');
        passwordInput.focus();
        return;
      }
      
      if (password.length < 6) {
        showToast('warning', 'La contraseña debe tener al menos 6 caracteres');
        passwordInput.focus();
        return;
      }
      
      const fakeEmail = username + "@app.com";
      setLoading(true);
      
      try {
        const userCredential = await signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, fakeEmail, password);
        const uid = userCredential.user.uid;
        const userDoc = await getDoc(doc(db, "users", uid));
        
        if (!userDoc.exists()) {
          showToast('error', 'Usuario no registrado');
          setLoading(false);
          return;
        }
        
        const userData = userDoc.data();
        
        if (!userData.enabled) {
          showToast('error', 'Usuario no habilitado. Contacta al administrador');
          setLoading(false);
          return;
        }
        
        Swal.fire({
          icon: 'success',
          title: '¡Bienvenido!',
          text: `Hola ${userData.username || username}`,
          timer: 1500,
          showConfirmButton: false
        });
        
        setTimeout(() => {
          window.location.href = "index.html";
        }, 1500);
        
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Login error:', err);
        
        let errorMessage = 'Credenciales incorrectas';
        
        switch (err.code) {
          case 'auth/user-not-found':
            errorMessage = 'Usuario no encontrado';
            break;
          case 'auth/wrong-password':
            errorMessage = 'Contraseña incorrecta';
            break;
          case 'auth/invalid-email':
            errorMessage = 'Formato de usuario inválido';
            break;
          case 'auth/too-many-requests':
            errorMessage = 'Demasiados intentos. Espera un momento';
            break;
          case 'auth/network-request-failed':
            errorMessage = 'Error de conexión. Verifica internet';
            break;
          default:
            errorMessage = 'Error al iniciar sesión';
        }
        
        showToast('error', errorMessage);
        setLoading(false);
        passwordInput.value = '';
      }
    }
    
    loginForm.addEventListener('submit', handleLogin);
    
    usernameInput.addEventListener('keypress', (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Enter') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (usernameInput.value && !passwordInput.value) {
          passwordInput.focus();
        } else if (usernameInput.value && passwordInput.value) {
          handleLogin(e);
        }
      }
    });
    
    passwordInput.addEventListener('keypress', (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Enter') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (usernameInput.value && passwordInput.value) {
          handleLogin(e);
        }
      }
    });
    
    console.log('%c🚀 Sistema de Control de Agencias v2.5', 'color: #4f46e5; font-size: 14px; font-weight: bold;');
  </script>
</body>
</html>

ARCHIVO: .\network.js

// network.js - Versión módulo
export function initNetworkStatus() {
  const statusEl = document.getElementById('networkStatus');
  
  function updateNetworkStatus() {
    if (!statusEl) return;
    if (navigator.onLine) {
      statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-emerald-500 rounded-full animate-pulse"></span> Online';
    } else {
      statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-red-500 rounded-full"></span> Offline';
    }
  }
  
  window.addEventListener('online', updateNetworkStatus);
  window.addEventListener('offline', updateNetworkStatus);
  updateNetworkStatus();
}

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="es">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <title>Registro</title>

  <!-- Tailwind -->
  <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>

  <!-- SweetAlert2 -->
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/sweetalert2@11"></script>

</head>

<body class="bg-gray-100 flex items-center justify-center h-screen">

  <!-- Card -->
  <div class="bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-lg w-full max-w-sm">

    <h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-center text-gray-800 mb-6">
      Crear Cuenta
    </h2>

    <!-- Usuario -->
    <input 
      type="text" 
      id="username" 
      placeholder="Usuario"
      class="w-full mb-4 px-4 py-2 border rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-green-500"
    >

    <!-- Contraseña -->
    <input 
      type="password" 
      id="password" 
      placeholder="Contraseña"
      class="w-full mb-4 px-4 py-2 border rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-green-500"
    >

    <!-- Confirmar contraseña -->
    <input 
      type="password" 
      id="confirmPassword" 
      placeholder="Confirmar contraseña"
      class="w-full mb-6 px-4 py-2 border rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-green-500"
    >

    <!-- Botón -->
    <button 
      onclick="register()"
      class="w-full bg-green-500 hover:bg-green-600 text-white font-semibold py-2 rounded-lg transition duration-200"
    >
      Registrarse
    </button>

    <!-- Login -->
    <p class="text-sm text-center mt-4">
      ¿Ya tienes cuenta?
      <a href="login.html" class="text-blue-500 hover:underline">
        Iniciar sesión
      </a>
    </p>

  </div>

  <!-- SCRIPT -->
  <script type="module">

    import { auth, db } from "./js/firebase/firebase.init.js";

    import {
      createUserWithEmailAndPassword
    } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-auth.js";

    import {
      doc,
      setDoc
    } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-firestore.js";

    async function register(){

      const username = document.getElementById("username").value.trim();
      const password = document.getElementById("password").value.trim();
      const confirmPassword = document.getElementById("confirmPassword").value.trim();

      // Validaciones
      if(!username || !password || !confirmPassword){
        Swal.fire({
          toast:true,
          position:"top-end",
          icon:"warning",
          title:"Completa todos los campos",
          showConfirmButton:false,
          timer:2000
        });
        return;
      }

      if(password !== confirmPassword){
        Swal.fire({
          toast:true,
          position:"top-end",
          icon:"error",
          title:"Las contraseñas no coinciden",
          showConfirmButton:false,
          timer:2500
        });
        return;
      }

      if(password.length < 6){
        Swal.fire({
          toast:true,
          position:"top-end",
          icon:"warning",
          title:"Mínimo 6 caracteres",
          showConfirmButton:false,
          timer:2500
        });
        return;
      }

      const fakeEmail = username + "@app.com";

      try{

        const userCredential = await createUserWithEmailAndPassword(
          auth,
          fakeEmail,
          password
        );

        const uid = userCredential.user.uid;

        // Guardar en Firestore
        await setDoc(doc(db,"users",uid),{
          username: username,
          enabled: false, // 🔥 tú lo habilitas
          createdAt: new Date()
        });

        Swal.fire({
          icon:"success",
          title:"Registro exitoso",
          text:"Espera que el administrador te habilite"
        });

        // Redirigir al login
        setTimeout(()=>{
          window.location.href = "login.html";
        },1500);

      }catch(err){

        let mensaje = "Error al registrar";

        if(err.code === "auth/email-already-in-use"){
          mensaje = "El usuario ya existe";
        }

        Swal.fire({
          icon:"error",
          title:"Error",
          text: mensaje
        });

      }

    }

    window.register = register;

  </script>

</body>
</html>

ARCHIVO: .\admin.html
================================================================================
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="es">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <title>Admin</title>

  <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/sweetalert2@11"></script>

</head>

<body class="bg-gray-100">

  <!-- NAVBAR -->
  <div class="bg-white shadow p-4 flex justify-between items-center">

    <h1 class="font-bold text-lg">Panel Admin</h1>

    <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
      <span id="userName" class="text-gray-600"></span>

      <button onclick="logout()"
        class="bg-red-500 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded hover:bg-red-600">
        Cerrar sesión
      </button>
    </div>

  </div>

  <!-- CONTENIDO -->
  <div class="p-6">

    <!-- BUSCADOR -->
    <input 
      type="text" 
      id="search"
      placeholder="Buscar usuario..."
      class="w-full mb-4 p-2 border rounded"
    >

    <!-- TABLA -->
    <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-4 overflow-x-auto">

      <table class="w-full text-left">
        <thead>
          <tr class="border-b text-gray-600">
            <th class="p-2">Usuario</th>
            <th class="p-2">Estado</th>
            <th class="p-2">Acciones</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody id="usersTable"></tbody>
      </table>

      <p id="loading" class="text-center text-gray-400 mt-4">
        Cargando usuarios...
      </p>

    </div>

  </div>

  <!-- AUTH GUARD -->
  <script type="module">
    import { requireAdmin } from "./js/auth.guard.js";
    requireAdmin();
  </script>

  <!-- SCRIPT -->
  <script type="module">

    import { db, auth } from "./js/firebase/firebase.init.js";

    import {
      collection,
      getDocs,
      doc,
      updateDoc,
      deleteDoc
    } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-firestore.js";

    import {
      onAuthStateChanged,
      signOut
    } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-auth.js";

    const table = document.getElementById("usersTable");
    const searchInput = document.getElementById("search");
    const loading = document.getElementById("loading");

    let users = [];

    // 🔥 Mostrar usuario
    onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user)=>{
      if(user){
        document.getElementById("userName").innerText =
          user.email.replace("@app.com","");
      }
    });

    // 🚀 Cargar usuarios
    async function loadUsers(){

      loading.style.display = "block";
      table.innerHTML = "";

      const snapshot = await getDocs(collection(db,"users"));

      users = [];

      snapshot.forEach(docSnap => {
        users.push({
          id: docSnap.id,
          ...docSnap.data()
        });
      });

      renderUsers(users);

      loading.style.display = "none";
    }

    // 🎯 Render
    function renderUsers(list){

      table.innerHTML = "";

      list.forEach(user => {

        table.innerHTML += `
          <tr class="border-b hover:bg-gray-50">
            <td class="p-2">${user.username}</td>

            <td class="p-2">
              <span class="px-2 py-1 rounded text-white text-sm
                ${user.enabled ? "bg-green-500" : "bg-red-500"}">
                ${user.enabled ? "Activo" : "Inactivo"}
              </span>
            </td>

            <td class="p-2 flex gap-2">

              <button onclick="toggleUser('${user.id}', ${user.enabled})"
                class="bg-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-600 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded">
                ${user.enabled ? "Desactivar" : "Activar"}
              </button>

              <button onclick="deleteUser('${user.id}')"
                class="bg-red-500 hover:bg-red-600 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded">
                Eliminar
              </button>

            </td>
          </tr>
        `;
      });

    }

    // 🔍 BUSCADOR
    searchInput.addEventListener("input", ()=>{

      const value = searchInput.value.toLowerCase();

      const filtered = users.filter(u =>
        u.username.toLowerCase().includes(value)
      );

      renderUsers(filtered);
    });

    // 🔄 Toggle
    async function toggleUser(id, current){

      await updateDoc(doc(db,"users",id),{
        enabled: !current
      });

      Swal.fire({
        toast:true,
        position:"top-end",
        icon:"success",
        title:"Usuario actualizado",
        showConfirmButton:false,
        timer:2000
      });

      loadUsers();
    }

    // ❌ Delete
    async function deleteUser(id){

      const confirm = await Swal.fire({
        title:"¿Eliminar usuario?",
        icon:"warning",
        showCancelButton:true
      });

      if(confirm.isConfirmed){

        await deleteDoc(doc(db,"users",id));

        Swal.fire("Eliminado","","success");

        loadUsers();
      }

    }

    // 🚪 Logout
    async function logout(){
      await signOut(auth);
      window.location.href = "login.html";
    }

    // GLOBAL
    window.toggleUser = toggleUser;
    window.deleteUser = deleteUser;
    window.logout = logout;

    loadUsers();

  </script>

</body>
</html>

<script>
    (function() {
      const statusEl = document.getElementById('networkStatus');
      if (!statusEl) return;
      
      function updateNetworkStatus() {
        if (navigator.onLine) {
          statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-emerald-500 rounded-full animate-pulse"></span> Online';
        } else {
          statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-red-500 rounded-full"></span> Offline';
        }
      }
      
      window.addEventListener('online', updateNetworkStatus);
      window.addEventListener('offline', updateNetworkStatus);
      updateNetworkStatus();
    })();
  </script>

ARCHIVO: .\dashboard.html
================================================================================
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="es">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <title>Dashboard</title>

  <link rel="icon" href="assets/icons/Logo-vertical-azul.png" />
  <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.5.0/css/all.min.css" />
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/sweetalert2@11"></script>

</head>
<body class="bg-gray-100 p-4">

  <h1 class="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">
    <i class="fas fa-chart-line text-red-600"></i>
    Dashboard de Hallazgos

            >

  <script type="module">
    import { exportAgenciesAdvanced, importAgenciesAdvanced } from './js/agencies/agencies.store.js';
    
    const btnExport = document.getElementById('btnExport');
    const btnImport = document.getElementById('btnImport');
    const fileImport = document.getElementById('fileImport');

    // Función de ayuda para notificaciones tipo Toast
    const Toast = Swal.mixin({
      toast: true,
      position: 'top-end',
      showConfirmButton: false,
      timer: 3000,
      timerProgressBar: true
    });

    // Acción Exportar
    btnExport.onclick = async () => {
      try {
        await exportAgenciesAdvanced();
        Toast.fire({ icon: 'success', title: 'Archivo exportado correctamente' });
      } catch (err) {
        Toast.fire({ icon: 'error', title: 'Error al exportar' });
      }
    };
    
    // Acción Abrir Selector de Archivos
    btnImport.onclick = () => fileImport.click();
    
    // Acción Importar al cambiar archivo
    fileImport.addEventListener('change', async (e) => {
      const file = e.target.files[0];
      if (file) {
        try {
          await importAgenciesAdvanced(file);
          Swal.fire({
            icon: 'success',
            title: '¡Importación exitosa!',
            text: 'Los datos han sido cargados.',
            confirmButtonColor: '#2563eb'
          });
        } catch (err) {
          Swal.fire({
            icon: 'error',
            title: 'Fallo la carga',
            text: 'El archivo JSON no es válido.'
          });
        }
        // Limpiar para poder subir el mismo archivo después si se desea
        fileImport.value = '';
      }
    });
  </script>

</body>
</html>

                            <span class="text-3xl block mb-2">🧺</span>
                            <span class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700">Ace</span>

    <script>
let registros = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("entregas")) || [];
let editIndex = null; // Para saber si estamos editando o creando

async function guardar() {
    const agenciaInput = document.getElementById("agencia");
    const agencia = agenciaInput.value;

    const data = {
        agencia,
        mistolin: document.getElementById("mistolin").checked,
        cloro: document.getElementById("cloro").checked,
        ace: document.getElementById("ace").checked,
        toallita: document.getElementById("toallita").checked,
        fecha: new Date().toLocaleString('es-DO', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', day: '2-digit', month: 'short' })
    };

    // Validación con SweetAlert2
    if (!agencia) {
        Swal.fire({
            icon: 'error',
            title: 'Faltan datos',
            text: 'Por favor, ingresa el nombre de la agencia.',
            confirmButtonColor: '#4f46e5'
        });
        return;
    }

    if (!data.mistolin && !data.cloro && !data.ace && !data.toallita) {
        Swal.fire({
            icon: 'warning',
            title: 'Carrito vacío',
            text: 'Selecciona al menos un producto para registrar.',
            confirmButtonColor: '#4f46e5'
        });
        return;
    }

    // Lógica para Guardar o Actualizar
    if (editIndex !== null) {
        registros[editIndex] = data;
        editIndex = null;
        Swal.fire({
            icon: 'success',
            title: '¡Actualizado!',
            text: 'El registro se modificó correctamente.',
            timer: 1500,
            showConfirmButton: false
        });
    } else {
        registros.push(data);
        Swal.fire({
            icon: 'success',
            title: '¡Registrado!',
            text: 'La entrega ha sido guardada.',
            timer: 1500,
            showConfirmButton: false
        });
    }

    localStorage.setItem("entregas", JSON.stringify(registros));
    render();
    limpiar();
}

function prepararEdicion(index) {
    // Buscamos el registro (considerando que el render está invertido)
    const actualIndex = registros.length - 1 - index;
    const r = registros[actualIndex];

    document.getElementById("agencia").value = r.agencia;
    document.getElementById("mistolin").checked = r.mistolin;
    document.getElementById("cloro").checked = r.cloro;
    document.getElementById("ace").checked = r.ace;
    document.getElementById("toallita").checked = r.toallita;

    editIndex = actualIndex;
    
    // Scroll hacia arriba para que el usuario vea que está editando
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
}

function eliminarRegistro(index) {
    const actualIndex = registros.length - 1 - index;
    
    Swal.fire({
        title: '¿Estás seguro?',
        text: "No podrás revertir esta acción",
        icon: 'warning',
        showCancelButton: true,
        confirmButtonColor: '#ef4444',
        cancelButtonColor: '#64748b',
        confirmButtonText: 'Sí, eliminar',
        cancelButtonText: 'Cancelar'
    }).then((result) => {
        if (result.isConfirmed) {
            registros.splice(actualIndex, 1);
            localStorage.setItem("entregas", JSON.stringify(registros));
            render();
            Swal.fire('Eliminado', 'El registro ha sido borrado.', 'success');
        }
    });
}

function render() {
    const lista = document.getElementById("lista");
    lista.innerHTML = "";
    let total = { mistolin: 0, cloro: 0, ace: 0, toallita: 0, kits: 0 };

    registros.slice().reverse().forEach((r, index) => {
        if (r.mistolin) total.mistolin++;
        if (r.cloro) total.cloro++;
        if (r.ace) total.ace++;
        if (r.toallita) total.toallita++;
        if (r.mistolin && r.cloro && r.ace && r.toallita) total.kits++;

        lista.innerHTML += `
            <div class="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-slate-100 flex justify-between items-center">
                <div onclick="prepararEdicion(${index})" class="cursor-pointer flex-1">
                    <div class="font-bold text-slate-800">${r.agencia} <span class="text-indigo-500 text-[10px] ml-2">✎ Editar</span></div>
                    <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-medium uppercase mt-1">${r.fecha}</div>
                    <div class="flex gap-1 mt-2">
                        ${r.mistolin ? "<span>🧴</span>" : ""} 
                        ${r.cloro ? "<span>🧪</span>" : ""} 
                        ${r.ace ? "<span>🧺</span>" : ""} 
                        ${r.toallita ? "<span>🧻</span>" : ""}
                    </div>
                </div>
                <button onclick="eliminarRegistro(${index})" class="ml-4 text-slate-300 hover:text-red-500 transition-colors">
                    <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="h-5 w-5" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
                        <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16" />
                    </svg>
                </button>
            </div>
        `;
    });

    // Actualizar números del dashboard
    document.getElementById("r_mistolin").textContent = total.mistolin;
    document.getElementById("r_cloro").textContent = total.cloro;
    document.getElementById("r_ace").textContent = total.ace;
    document.getElementById("r_toallita").textContent = total.toallita;
    document.getElementById("r_kits").textContent = total.kits;
}

function limpiar() {
    document.getElementById("agencia").value = "";
    document.querySelectorAll(".product-check").forEach(c => c.checked = false);
    editIndex = null;
}

render();
    </script>
</body>
</html>

  <script>
    const statusEl = document.getElementById('networkStatus');
    function updateNetworkStatus() {
      if (!statusEl) return;
      if (navigator.onLine) {
        statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-emerald-500 rounded-full animate-pulse"></span> Online';
      } else {
        statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-red-500 rounded-full"></span> Offline';
      }
    }
    window.addEventListener('online', updateNetworkStatus);
    window.addEventListener('offline', updateNetworkStatus);
    updateNetworkStatus();
  </script>

    import { signOut } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-auth.js";
    
    window.logout = async () => {
      await signOut(auth);
      window.location.href = "login.html";
    };
  </script>

  <!-- Menú móvil -->
  <script>
    (function() {
      const mobileMenu = document.getElementById('mobileMenu');
      const openBtn = document.getElementById('mobileMenuBtn');
      const closeBtn = document.getElementById('closeMenuBtn');
      
      if (openBtn && mobileMenu) {
        openBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          mobileMenu.classList.remove('hidden');
          document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        });
        
        const closeMenu = () => {
          mobileMenu.classList.add('hidden');
          document.body.style.overflow = '';
        };
        
        if (closeBtn) closeBtn.addEventListener('click', closeMenu);
        mobileMenu.addEventListener('click', (e) => { if (e.target === mobileMenu) closeMenu(); });
      }
    })();
  </script>

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="es">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, user-scalable=no">
  <title>Login | Control de Agencias</title>

  <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/sweetalert2@11"></script>

  <style>
    @import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@300;400;500;600;700&display=swap');
    
    * { margin: 0; padding: 0; box-sizing: border-box; }
    
    body {
      font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #667eea 0%, #764ba2 100%);
      min-height: 100vh;
    }
    
    @keyframes fadeInUp {
      from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(30px); }
      to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); }
    }
    
    .login-card { animation: fadeInUp 0.6s ease-out; }
    
    .input-focus:focus {
      box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.2);
      border-color: #4f46e5;
    }
    
    .loader {
      display: inline-block;
      width: 20px;
      height: 20px;
      border: 2px solid #fff;
      border-radius: 50%;
      border-top-color: transparent;
      animation: spin 0.6s linear infinite;
    }
    
    @keyframes spin { to { transform: rotate(360deg); } }
  </style>
</head>

<body class="flex items-center justify-center p-4">
  <div class="login-card w-full max-w-md">
    
    <div class="text-center mb-8">
      <div class="w-20 h-20 bg-white/10 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4 backdrop-blur">
        <svg class="w-12 h-12 text-white" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5" d="M19 21V5a2 2 0 00-2-2H7a2 2 0 00-2 2v16m14 0h2m-2 0h-5m-9 0H3m2 0h5M9 7h1m-1 4h1m4-4h1m-1 4h1m-5 10v-5a1 1 0 011-1h2a1 1 0 011 1v5m-4 0h4"></path>
        </svg>
      </div>
      <h1 class="text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-1">Control de Agencias</h1>
      <p class="text-indigo-200 text-sm">Inicia sesión para continuar</p>
    </div>

    <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl p-8">
      <h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800 text-center mb-6">Bienvenido</h2>

      <form id="loginForm" class="space-y-5">
        <div>
          <label class="block text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 mb-2">
            <i class="fas fa-user mr-2 text-indigo-500"></i>
            Usuario
          </label>
          <input 
            type="text" 
            id="username" 
            name="username"
            autocomplete="username"
            placeholder="Ingresa tu usuario"
            class="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-gray-300 rounded-xl focus:outline-none input-focus transition-all text-gray-700"
            autofocus
          >
        </div>

        <div>
          <label class="block text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 mb-2">
            <i class="fas fa-lock mr-2 text-indigo-500"></i>
            Contraseña
          </label>
          <div class="relative">
            <input 
              type="password" 
              id="password" 
              name="password"
              autocomplete="current-password"
              placeholder="••••••"
              class="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-gray-300 rounded-xl focus:outline-none input-focus transition-all text-gray-700 pr-12"
            >
            <button type="button" id="togglePassword" class="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 hover:text-indigo-600 transition-colors">
              <i class="fas fa-eye-slash" id="toggleIcon"></i>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <button type="submit" id="loginBtn" class="w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-indigo-700 hover:from-indigo-700 hover:to-indigo-800 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl transition-all transform active:scale-95 shadow-lg shadow-indigo-200 flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
          <i class="fas fa-sign-in-alt"></i>
          <span>Iniciar Sesión</span>
        </button>

        <div class="relative my-6">
          <div class="absolute inset-0 flex items-center">
            <div class="w-full border-t border-gray-200"></div>
          </div>
          <div class="relative flex justify-center text-sm">
            <span class="px-4 bg-white text-gray-400">¿Nuevo usuario?</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <a href="register.html" class="block text-center text-indigo-600 hover:text-indigo-700 font-semibold transition-colors">
          Crear una cuenta
        </a>
      </form>
    </div>

    <p class="text-center text-indigo-200 text-xs mt-6">Sistema de Control de Agencias v2.5</p>
  </div>

  <script type="module">
    import { auth, db } from "./js/firebase/firebase.init.js";

    import {
      signInWithEmailAndPassword,
      onAuthStateChanged
    } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-auth.js";

    import {
      doc,
      getDoc
    } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-firestore.js";

    // ============================================
    // 🧹 LIMPIEZA INTELIGENTE DE DATOS LOCALES
    // ============================================
    async function limpiarDatosLocales() {
      console.log('🧹 Limpiando cachés locales...');
      
      // 1. Limpiar localStorage (excepto configuraciones importantes)
      const keepKeys = ['user_preferences', 'theme']; // Cosas que queremos conservar
      const keysToDelete = [];
      
      for (let i = 0; i < localStorage.length; i++) {
        const key = localStorage.key(i);
        if (!keepKeys.includes(key)) {
          keysToDelete.push(key);
        }
      }
      
      keysToDelete.forEach(key => {
        localStorage.removeItem(key);
        console.log(`   - Eliminado localStorage: ${key}`);
      });
      
      // 2. Limpiar sessionStorage completamente
      if (sessionStorage.length > 0) {
        sessionStorage.clear();
        console.log('   - SessionStorage limpiado');
      }
      
      // 3. Limpiar IndexedDB (solo cachés, NO datos de agencias)
      try {
        // Abrir la base de datos
        const request = indexedDB.open('control_agencias_db');
        
        request.onsuccess = (event) => {
          const db = event.target.result;
          
          // Lista de stores a limpiar (NO 'agencies' ni 'observaciones')
          const storesToClear = ['sync_queue', 'findings', 'reports', 'visits', 'observations', 'routes'];
          
          storesToClear.forEach(storeName => {
            if (db.objectStoreNames.contains(storeName)) {
              const transaction = db.transaction(storeName, 'readwrite');
              const store = transaction.objectStore(storeName);
              const clearRequest = store.clear();
              
              clearRequest.onsuccess = () => {
                console.log(`   - IndexedDB: ${storeName} limpiado`);
              };
              clearRequest.onerror = (err) => {
                console.warn(`   - Error limpiando ${storeName}:`, err);
              };
            }
          });
          
          db.close();
        };
        
        request.onerror = (err) => {
          console.warn('   - Error abriendo IndexedDB:', err);
        };
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('   - IndexedDB no disponible:', err);
      }
      
      // 4. Limpiar caché de mapas (si existe)
      if ('caches' in window) {
        try {
          const cacheNames = await caches.keys();
          for (const cacheName of cacheNames) {
            if (cacheName.includes('leaflet') || cacheName.includes('tile')) {
              await caches.delete(cacheName);
              console.log(`   - Caché de mapas eliminado: ${cacheName}`);
            }
          }
        } catch (err) {
          console.warn('   - Error limpiando cachés:', err);
        }
      }
      
      console.log('✅ Limpieza de cachés completada');
    }
    
    // Ejecutar limpieza al cargar la página
    await limpiarDatosLocales();
    
    // Mostrar mensaje de confirmación (opcional, solo si hay algo que limpiar)
    const toast = Swal.mixin({
      toast: true,
      position: 'top-end',
      showConfirmButton: false,
      timer: 2000,
      timerProgressBar: true
    });
    
    toast.fire({
      icon: 'info',
      title: 'Caché limpiado',
      text: 'Datos temporales eliminados'
    });
    
    // ============================================
    // LOGIN NORMAL
    // ============================================
    
    const usernameInput = document.getElementById('username');
    const passwordInput = document.getElementById('password');
    const loginBtn = document.getElementById('loginBtn');
    const loginForm = document.getElementById('loginForm');
    const togglePasswordBtn = document.getElementById('togglePassword');
    const toggleIcon = document.getElementById('toggleIcon');

    let isLoading = false;

    onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user) => {
      if (user && !isLoading) {
        window.location.href = "index.html";
      }
    });

    togglePasswordBtn?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const type = passwordInput.getAttribute('type') === 'password' ? 'text' : 'password';
      passwordInput.setAttribute('type', type);
      toggleIcon.className = type === 'password' ? 'fas fa-eye-slash' : 'fas fa-eye';
    });

    function sanitizeInput(str) {
      if (!str) return '';
      return str.trim().replace(/[<>]/g, '');
    }

    function setLoading(loading) {
      isLoading = loading;
      if (loading) {
        loginBtn.disabled = true;
        loginBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="loader"></span><span class="ml-2">Verificando...</span>';
      } else {
        loginBtn.disabled = false;
        loginBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-sign-in-alt"></i><span>Iniciar Sesión</span>';
      }
    }

    function showToast(icon, title) {
      Swal.fire({
        toast: true,
        position: "top-end",
        icon: icon,
        title: title,
        showConfirmButton: false,
        timer: 2500,
        timerProgressBar: true
      });
    }

    async function handleLogin(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      
      if (isLoading) return;
      
      let username = sanitizeInput(usernameInput.value);
      let password = sanitizeInput(passwordInput.value);
      
      if (!username) {
        showToast('warning', 'Ingresa tu usuario');
        usernameInput.focus();
        return;
      }
      
      if (!password) {
        showToast('warning', 'Ingresa tu contraseña');
        passwordInput.focus();
        return;
      }
      
      if (password.length < 6) {
        showToast('warning', 'La contraseña debe tener al menos 6 caracteres');
        passwordInput.focus();
        return;
      }
      
      const fakeEmail = username + "@app.com";
      setLoading(true);
      
      try {
        const userCredential = await signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, fakeEmail, password);
        const uid = userCredential.user.uid;
        const userDoc = await getDoc(doc(db, "users", uid));
        
        if (!userDoc.exists()) {
          showToast('error', 'Usuario no registrado');
          setLoading(false);
          return;
        }
        
        const userData = userDoc.data();
        
        if (!userData.enabled) {
          showToast('error', 'Usuario no habilitado. Contacta al administrador');
          setLoading(false);
          return;
        }
        
        Swal.fire({
          icon: 'success',
          title: '¡Bienvenido!',
          text: `Hola ${userData.username || username}`,
          timer: 1500,
          showConfirmButton: false
        });
        
        setTimeout(() => {
          window.location.href = "index.html";
        }, 1500);
        
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Login error:', err);
        
        let errorMessage = 'Credenciales incorrectas';
        
        switch (err.code) {
          case 'auth/user-not-found':
            errorMessage = 'Usuario no encontrado';
            break;
          case 'auth/wrong-password':
            errorMessage = 'Contraseña incorrecta';
            break;
          case 'auth/invalid-email':
            errorMessage = 'Formato de usuario inválido';
            break;
          case 'auth/too-many-requests':
            errorMessage = 'Demasiados intentos. Espera un momento';
            break;
          case 'auth/network-request-failed':
            errorMessage = 'Error de conexión. Verifica internet';
            break;
          default:
            errorMessage = 'Error al iniciar sesión';
        }
        
        showToast('error', errorMessage);
        setLoading(false);
        passwordInput.value = '';
      }
    }
    
    loginForm.addEventListener('submit', handleLogin);
    
    usernameInput.addEventListener('keypress', (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Enter') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (usernameInput.value && !passwordInput.value) {
          passwordInput.focus();
        } else if (usernameInput.value && passwordInput.value) {
          handleLogin(e);
        }
      }
    });
    
    passwordInput.addEventListener('keypress', (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Enter') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (usernameInput.value && passwordInput.value) {
          handleLogin(e);
        }
      }
    });
    
    console.log('%c🚀 Sistema de Control de Agencias v2.5', 'color: #4f46e5; font-size: 14px; font-weight: bold;');
  </script>
</body>
</html>

ARCHIVO: .\network.js

// network.js - Versión módulo
export function initNetworkStatus() {
  const statusEl = document.getElementById('networkStatus');
  
  function updateNetworkStatus() {
    if (!statusEl) return;
    if (navigator.onLine) {
      statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-emerald-500 rounded-full animate-pulse"></span> Online';
    } else {
      statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-red-500 rounded-full"></span> Offline';
    }
  }
  
  window.addEventListener('online', updateNetworkStatus);
  window.addEventListener('offline', updateNetworkStatus);
  updateNetworkStatus();
}

<!DOCTYPE html>

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="es">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <title>Registro</title>

  <!-- Tailwind -->
  <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>

  <!-- SweetAlert2 -->
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/sweetalert2@11"></script>

</head>

<body class="bg-gray-100 flex items-center justify-center h-screen">

  <!-- Card -->
  <div class="bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-lg w-full max-w-sm">

    <h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-center text-gray-800 mb-6">
      Crear Cuenta
    </h2>

    <!-- Usuario -->
    <input 
      type="text" 
      id="username" 
      placeholder="Usuario"
      class="w-full mb-4 px-4 py-2 border rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-green-500"
    >

    <!-- Contraseña -->
    <input 
      type="password" 
      id="password" 
      placeholder="Contraseña"
      class="w-full mb-4 px-4 py-2 border rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-green-500"
    >

    <!-- Confirmar contraseña -->
    <input 
      type="password" 
      id="confirmPassword" 
      placeholder="Confirmar contraseña"
      class="w-full mb-6 px-4 py-2 border rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-green-500"
    >

    <!-- Botón -->
    <button 
      onclick="register()"
      class="w-full bg-green-500 hover:bg-green-600 text-white font-semibold py-2 rounded-lg transition duration-200"
    >
      Registrarse
    </button>

    <!-- Login -->
    <p class="text-sm text-center mt-4">
      ¿Ya tienes cuenta?
      <a href="login.html" class="text-blue-500 hover:underline">
        Iniciar sesión
      </a>
    </p>

  </div>

  <!-- SCRIPT -->
  <script type="module">

    import { auth, db } from "./js/firebase/firebase.init.js";

    import {
      createUserWithEmailAndPassword
    } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-auth.js";

    import {
      doc,
      setDoc
    } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-firestore.js";

    async function register(){

      const username = document.getElementById("username").value.trim();
      const password = document.getElementById("password").value.trim();
      const confirmPassword = document.getElementById("confirmPassword").value.trim();

      // Validaciones
      if(!username || !password || !confirmPassword){
        Swal.fire({
          toast:true,
          position:"top-end",
          icon:"warning",
          title:"Completa todos los campos",
          showConfirmButton:false,
          timer:2000
        });
        return;
      }

      if(password !== confirmPassword){
        Swal.fire({
          toast:true,
          position:"top-end",
          icon:"error",
          title:"Las contraseñas no coinciden",
          showConfirmButton:false,
          timer:2500
        });
        return;
      }

      if(password.length < 6){
        Swal.fire({
          toast:true,
          position:"top-end",
          icon:"warning",
          title:"Mínimo 6 caracteres",
          showConfirmButton:false,
          timer:2500
        });
        return;
      }

      const fakeEmail = username + "@app.com";

      try{

        const userCredential = await createUserWithEmailAndPassword(
          auth,
          fakeEmail,
          password
        );

        const uid = userCredential.user.uid;

        // Guardar en Firestore
        await setDoc(doc(db,"users",uid),{
          username: username,
          enabled: false, // 🔥 tú lo habilitas
          createdAt: new Date()
        });

        Swal.fire({
          icon:"success",
          title:"Registro exitoso",
          text:"Espera que el administrador te habilite"
        });

        // Redirigir al login
        setTimeout(()=>{
          window.location.href = "login.html";
        },1500);

      }catch(err){

        let mensaje = "Error al registrar";

        if(err.code === "auth/email-already-in-use"){
          mensaje = "El usuario ya existe";
        }

        Swal.fire({
          icon:"error",
          title:"Error",
          text: mensaje
        });

      }

    }

    window.register = register;

  </script>

</body>
</html>

ARCHIVO: .\unir.py
================================================================================
import os

OUTPUT_FILE = "todo_el_proyecto.txt"

EXCLUDE_DIRS = {
    ".git",
    "node_modules",
    "__pycache__",
    "venv",
    "env",
    "assets",
    "vendor"
}

EXCLUDE_FILES = {
    "tailwindcss.js",
    "idb.min.js"
}

with open(OUTPUT_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out:
    for root, dirs, files in os.walk("."):
        dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in EXCLUDE_DIRS]

        for file in files:
            if file in EXCLUDE_FILES:
                continue

            path = os.path.join(root, file)

            try:
                with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
                    content = f.read()

                out.write("\n\n" + "=" * 80 + "\n")
                out.write(f"ARCHIVO: {path}\n")
                out.write("=" * 80 + "\n")
                out.write(content)

            except Exception as e:
                out.write(f"\n\n[ERROR LEYENDO {path}: {e}]\n")

print("✅ Archivos unidos en:", OUTPUT_FILE)

// js/app.js - Versión corregida para el mapa

import { getAgencies } from './agencies/agencies.store.js';
import { initMap, updateUserPosition } from './map/map.service.js';
import { agencyMarker } from './map/markers.service.js';
import { startLocationTracking } from './gps/location.service.js';
import { checkAgencies } from './gps/geofence.service.js';
import { renderAgenciesList } from './agencies/agencies.list.ui.js';
import { openRouteSelector } from './map/route-selector.js';

// Esperar a que el DOM esté listo
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async () => {
  const btnSelectRoute = document.getElementById('btn-select-route');
if (btnSelectRoute) {
  btnSelectRoute.addEventListener('click', () => {
    openRouteSelector();
  });
}
  console.log('🚀 Iniciando aplicación...');
  
  // Verificar que el contenedor del mapa existe
  const mapContainer = document.getElementById('map');
  if (!mapContainer) {
    console.error('❌ Contenedor #map no encontrado');
    return;
  }
  
  console.log('✅ Contenedor #map encontrado');
  
  // Inicializar mapa
  const map = initMap();
  if (!map) {
    console.error('❌ Error al inicializar el mapa');
    return;
  }
  
  console.log('✅ Mapa inicializado');
  
  // Cargar agencias
  const agencies = await getAgencies();
  console.log(`📋 ${agencies.length} agencias cargadas`);
  
  // Filtrar agencias con coordenadas
  const agenciesWithCoords = agencies.filter(a => a.lat != null && a.lng != null);
  console.log(`📍 ${agenciesWithCoords.length} agencias con coordenadas`);
  
  // Agregar marcadores
  agenciesWithCoords.forEach(agency => {
    agencyMarker(agency).addTo(map);
  });
  
  // Selects de zona (solo si existen)
  const selectZona1 = document.querySelector("#selectZona1");
  const selectZona2 = document.querySelector("#selectZona2");
  
  if (selectZona1 && selectZona2) {
    const zonasUnicas = [...new Set(agenciesWithCoords.map(a => a.zona))];
    zonasUnicas.forEach(zona => {
      const option1 = document.createElement("option");
      option1.value = zona;
      option1.textContent = zona;
      selectZona1.appendChild(option1);
      
      const option2 = document.createElement("option");
      option2.value = zona;
      option2.textContent = zona;
      selectZona2.appendChild(option2);
    });
    
    selectZona2.addEventListener("change", () => {
      const valor = selectZona2.value;
      if (!valor) return;
      
      // Limpiar capas excepto tile layers
      map.eachLayer(layer => {
        if (!(layer instanceof L.TileLayer)) {
          map.removeLayer(layer);
        }
      });
      
      // Filtrar y agregar marcadores
      agenciesWithCoords
        .filter(a => a.zona === valor)
        .forEach(agency => {
          agencyMarker(agency).addTo(map);
        });
    });
  }
  
  // Widget de agencia cercana
  const closestName = document.getElementById('closest-name');
  const closestDistance = document.getElementById('closest-distance-text');
  const accuracyValue = document.getElementById('accuracy-value');
  const speedValue = document.getElementById('speed-value');
  const accDot = document.getElementById('acc-dot');
  
  // Iniciar tracking GPS
  startLocationTracking(async (pos) => {
    // Actualizar posición del usuario en el mapa
    updateUserPosition(map, pos);
    
    // Actualizar UI de precisión
    if (accuracyValue && accDot && pos.accuracy !== null) {
      const acc = Math.round(pos.accuracy);
      accuracyValue.textContent = acc;
      
      accDot.classList.remove("bg-emerald-500", "bg-amber-500", "bg-red-500", "animate-pulse");
      if (acc < 20) {
        accDot.classList.add("bg-emerald-500");
      } else if (acc < 70) {
        accDot.classList.add("bg-amber-500");
      } else {
        accDot.classList.add("bg-red-500", "animate-pulse");
      }
    }
    
    // Actualizar velocidad
    if (speedValue && pos.speed !== null) {
      const speedKMH = (pos.speed * 3.6).toFixed(1);
      speedValue.textContent = speedKMH;
    }
    
    // Verificar agencias cercanas
    const closest = await checkAgencies(pos, agenciesWithCoords);
    
    if (closest && closestName && closestDistance) {
      closestName.textContent = `AG ${closest.idReal}`;
      closestDistance.textContent = `${closest.currentDist} Metros`;
    }
    
    // Renderizar lista (con throttle para no saturar)
    renderAgenciesList(pos);
  });
  
  // Botón de re-centrar
  const btnRecenter = document.getElementById('btn-recenter');
  if (btnRecenter) {
    btnRecenter.addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (navigator.geolocation) {
        navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(pos => {
          map.setView([pos.coords.latitude, pos.coords.longitude], 17);
        });
      }
    });
  }
  
  // Toggle de lista
  const btnToggle = document.getElementById('btn-toggle-list');
  const btnClose = document.getElementById('btn-close-list');
  const listContainer = document.getElementById('collapsible-list');
  
  if (btnToggle && listContainer) {
    btnToggle.addEventListener('click', () => {
      listContainer.classList.toggle('is-active');
      const icon = btnToggle.querySelector('i');
      if (icon) {
        icon.className = listContainer.classList.contains('is-active') 
          ? 'fas fa-chevron-down' 
          : 'fas fa-list-ul';
      }
    });
  }
  
  if (btnClose && listContainer) {
    btnClose.addEventListener('click', () => {
      listContainer.classList.remove('is-active');
      const icon = btnToggle?.querySelector('i');
      if (icon) icon.className = 'fas fa-list-ul';
    });
  }
  
  // Renderizar lista inicial
  await renderAgenciesList();
  
  console.log('✅ Aplicación inicializada correctamente');
});

ARCHIVO: .\js\appzones.js
